 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">6.6. <U>Successors and Assigns</U>. This Agreement shall be binding upon and inure to the benefit of each
of the parties hereto and shall inure to the benefit of each of the parties hereto and their respective successors and assigns, and nothing herein is intended or shall be construed to give any other Person any right, remedy or claim under, to or in
respect of this Agreement or any Collateral. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">6.7. <U>Currency Conversions</U>. In calculating the amount of Secured Obligations for any
purpose hereunder, including voting or distribution purposes, the amount of any Secured Obligation which is denominated in a currency other than Dollars shall be converted into Dollars at the spot rate for purchasing Dollars with such currency as
set forth in <U>The Wall Street Journal</U> (or any successor or substitute publication reasonably selected by the Administrative Agent) on the Business Day prior to the date on which such calculation is to be made. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">6.8. <U>Governing Law</U>. This Agreement shall be governed by, and construed and interpreted in accordance with, the laws of the State of New York.
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">6.9. <U>Counterparts</U>. This Agreement may be signed (including by way of facsimile or electronic transmission) in any number of
counterparts with the same effect as if the signatures thereto and hereto were upon the same instrument. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">6.10. <U>Termination</U>.
(a)&nbsp;Upon (i)&nbsp;receipt by the Collateral Agent from the Administrative Agent of (A)&nbsp;a written direction to cause the Liens created by <U>Section&nbsp;4.7</U> and by the Security Documents to be released and discharged or (B)&nbsp;a
written notice stating that the Termination Date has occurred, and (ii)&nbsp;payment in full of all Collateral Agent Fees, the security interests created by <U>Section&nbsp;4.7</U> and by the Security Documents shall terminate forthwith and all
right, title and interest of the Collateral Agent in and to the Collateral shall revert to the Obligors, their successors and assigns. Notwithstanding anything to the contrary contained herein or in any Secured Instrument, the Lien of the holders of
the 2012 Notes created by any documents entered into pursuant to <U>clause (b)</U>&nbsp;of <U>Section&nbsp;6.3</U> and by the Security Documents shall be automatically and unconditionally released and discharged upon the release and discharge of the
Liens created by any documents entered into pursuant to <U>clause (b)</U>&nbsp;of <U>Section&nbsp;6.3</U> and by the Security Documents. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman"
SIZE="2">(b) For so long as no Event of Default shall have occurred and be continuing under the 2012 Indenture, upon receipt by the Collateral Agent of written notice, together with an Officer&#146;s Certificate and an Opinion of Counsel, from the
Borrower stating that the 2012 Notes are no longer required to be secured equally and ratably by the Liens securing the Credit Agreement Obligations pursuant to Section&nbsp;4.11 of the 2012 Notes Indenture, (which notice shall include evidence that
the Liens securing the Credit Agreement Obligations are &#147;Permitted Liens&#148; under the 2012 Notes Indenture), the security interests created by the Security Documents in favor of the Collateral Agent shall no longer secure the 2012 Notes and
the definition of &#147;Secured Obligations&#148; shall automatically be amended to no longer include the 2012 Notes without delivery of any instrument or performance of any act by any party. In the absence of bad faith or willful misconduct on its
part, the Collateral Agent may conclusively rely, as to the truth of the statements and the accuracy of the mathematical calculations stated therein, upon such written notice. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT
SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">18 </FONT></P>

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">(c) Upon the termination of the Collateral Agent&#146;s security interest and the release of the
Collateral in accordance with <U>clause (a)</U>&nbsp;of <U>Section&nbsp;6.10</U>, the Collateral Agent will promptly, at the Borrower&#146;s written request and expense, (i)&nbsp;execute and deliver to the Borrower such documents as the Borrower
shall reasonably request to evidence the termination of such security interest or the release of the Collateral and (ii)&nbsp;deliver or cause to be delivered to the Obligors all property of any Obligor then held by the Collateral Agent or any agent
thereof. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">(d) This Agreement shall terminate when the security interest granted under the Security Documents has terminated and the
Collateral has been released; provided that the provisions of <U>Sections 4.3</U>, <U>4.4</U>, <U>4.5</U> and <U>4.6</U> shall not be affected by any such termination. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">6.11. <U>New Obligors</U>. During the term of this Agreement, each Subsidiary of the Borrower that, pursuant to Section&nbsp;6.9 of the Credit Agreement, is required to become a party to this Agreement, may become
such a party by executing an assumption agreement, substantially in the form of <U>Exhibit A</U>, a copy of which shall be delivered to the Collateral Agent in accordance with <U>Section&nbsp;6.1</U>, whereupon such Subsidiary shall become an
Obligor for all purposes and to the same extent as if originally a party hereto and shall be bound by and entitled to the benefits of this Agreement. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">6.12. <U>Inconsistent Provisions</U>. If any provision of this Agreement shall be inconsistent with, or contrary to, any provision in any of the Security Documents or any document entered into in connection therewith,
the provisions of this Agreement shall be controlling and shall supersede such inconsistent provision to the extent necessary to give full effect to all provisions contained in this Agreement. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><U>6.13. Confidentiality</U> . The Collateral Agent agrees to keep confidential any written information (a)&nbsp;provided to it by or on behalf of the
Borrower or any of its Subsidiaries pursuant to or in connection with this Agreement or any Security Document or (b)&nbsp;obtained by the Collateral Agent based on a review of the books and records of the Borrower or any of its Subsidiaries;
<U>provided</U> that nothing herein shall prevent the Collateral Agent from disclosing any such information (i)&nbsp;to the Administrative Agent, any Lender, any Affiliate of a Lender, any Approved Fund or the Indenture Trustee, (ii)&nbsp;subject to
an agreement to comply with the provisions of this Section, to any actual or prospective Transferee or any direct or indirect counterparty to any Hedge Agreement (or any professional advisor to such counterparty), (iii)&nbsp;to its employees,
directors, agents, attorneys, accountants and other professional advisors or those of any of its affiliates having a need to know such information for purposes of allowing the Administrative Agent, Indenture Trustee, Collateral Agent or Lenders to
perform their obligations hereunder, (iii)&nbsp;upon the request or demand of any Governmental Authority having jurisdiction over the Collateral Agent or as shall be required pursuant to any requirement of law, provided the Borrower is given advance
written notice and only such information is disclosed as is necessary to comply with such request, demand or requirement, (iv)&nbsp;in response to any order of any court or other Governmental Authority or as may otherwise be required pursuant to any
requirement of law, provided the Borrower is given advance written notice and only such information is disclosed as is necessary to comply with such order, (v)&nbsp;if required to do so in </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">19 </FONT></P>

breach of this Agreement or (vii)&nbsp;to the extent reasonably necessary in connection with the exercise of any remedy hereunder. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">6.14.
<U>SUBMISSION TO JURISDICTION; WAIVERS</U>. EACH OBLIGOR HEREBY IRREVOCABLY AND UNCONDITIONALLY: </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">(a) SUBMITS FOR ITSELF AND ITS PROPERTY IN
ANY LEGAL ACTION OR PROCEEDING RELATING TO THIS AGREEMENT OR ANY SECURITY DOCUMENT TO WHICH IT IS A PARTY, OR FOR RECOGNITION AND ENFORCEMENT OF ANY JUDGMENT IN RESPECT THEREOF, TO THE NON EXCLUSIVE GENERAL JURISDICTION OF THE COURTS OF THE STATE OF
NEW YORK OR IN THE UNITED STATES OF AMERICA FOR THE SOUTHERN DISTRICT OF NEW YORK, AND APPELLATE COURTS FROM ANY THEREOF; </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">(b) TO THE
EXTENT PERMITTED BY APPLICABLE LAW, CONSENTS THAT ANY SUCH ACTION OR PROCEEDING MAY BE BROUGHT IN SUCH COURTS AND WAIVES ANY OBJECTION THAT IT MAY NOW OR HEREAFTER HAVE TO THE VENUE OF ANY SUCH ACTION OR PROCEEDING IN ANY SUCH COURT OR THAT SUCH
ACTION OR PROCEEDING WAS BROUGHT IN AN INCONVENIENT COURT AND AGREES NOT TO PLEAD OR CLAIM THE SAME; </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">(c) AGREES THAT SERVICE OF PROCESS IN
ANY SUCH ACTION OR PROCEEDING MAY BE EFFECTED BY MAILING A COPY THEREOF BY REGISTERED OR CERTIFIED MAIL (OR ANY SUBSTANTIALLY SIMILAR FORM OF MAIL), POSTAGE PREPAID, TO SUCH OBLIGOR AT ITS ADDRESS SPECIFIED IN <U>SUBSECTION 6.1</U> OR AT SUCH OTHER
ADDRESS OF WHICH THE COLLATERAL AGENT SHALL HAVE BEEN NOTIFIED PURSUANT THERETO; </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">(d) AGREES THAT NOTHING HEREIN SHALL AFFECT THE RIGHT TO
EFFECT SERVICE OF PROCESS IN ANY OTHER MANNER PERMITTED BY LAW OR SHALL LIMIT THE RIGHT TO SUE IN ANY OTHER JURISDICTION; AND </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">(e) WAIVES,
TO THE MAXIMUM EXTENT NOT PROHIBITED BY LAW, ANY RIGHT IT MAY HAVE TO CLAIM OR RECOVER IN ANY LEGAL ACTION OR PROCEEDING REFERRED TO IN THIS SUBSECTION ANY SPECIAL, EXEMPLARY, PUNITIVE OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">6.15. <U>WAIVER OF JURY TRIAL</U>. THE COLLATERAL AGENT AND EACH OF THE OBLIGORS HEREBY IRREVOCABLY AND UNCONDITIONALLY WAIVES TRIAL BY JURY IN ANY LEGAL
ACTION OR PROCEEDING RELATING TO THIS AGREEMENT OR ANY SECURITY DOCUMENT AND FOR ANY COUNTERCLAIM THEREIN. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">6.16. <U>PATRIOT ACT</U>. The
Collateral Agent hereby notifies each Obligor that, pursuant to the requirements of the PATRIOT Act, it is required to obtain, verify and record information that identifies each Obligor, which information includes the name and address of such
Obligor and other information that will allow the Collateral Agent to identify such Obligor </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>

FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">to Agreement </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">CERTAIN DEFINITIONS </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Unless otherwise defined herein, all capitalized terms used in this Agreement (including this <U>Schedule I</U>) shall have meanings given to them in the
Credit Agreement. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">The following terms shall have the respective meanings set forth below: </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>2012 Notes</U>&#148; &#150; as defined in the <U>second recital</U>. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>2012 Notes Indenture</U>&#148; &#150; as defined in the <U>second recital</U>. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Agreement</U>&#148; shall mean, on any date, this Collateral Trust Agreement as originally in effect on October&nbsp;24, 2006 and as thereafter
from time to time amended, supplemented, amended and restated or otherwise modified from time to time in accordance with its terms and in effect on such date. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Borrower</U>&#148; &#150; as defined in the <U>preamble</U>. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Collateral</U>&#148; shall
mean all property in which the Collateral Agent is granted a Lien from time to time under this Agreement or any other Security Document. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman"
SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Collateral Account</U>&#148; &#150; as defined in <U>Section&nbsp;3.1</U>. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Collateral Agent</U>&#148; shall
mean Wells Fargo Bank, N.A., in its capacity as collateral agent under this Agreement, and any successor collateral agent appointed thereunder. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman"
SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Collateral Agent Fees</U>&#148; shall mean all fees, costs and expenses of the Collateral Agent of the types described in <U>Sections 4.3</U>, <U>4.4</U>, <U>4.5</U> and <U>4.6</U> of this Agreement. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Consolidated Net Tangible Assets</U>&#148; &#150; as defined in the 2012 Notes Indenture. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Control Party</U>&#148; &#150; shall mean (a)&nbsp;at any time the amount of Credit Agreement Obligations is equal to or greater than the
outstanding principal amount of the 2012 Notes, the Administrative Agent and (b)&nbsp;at any time the outstanding principal amount of the 2012 Notes is greater than the amount of Credit Agreement Obligations, the Indenture Trustee. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Credit Agreement</U>&#148; &#150; as defined in the <U>first recital</U>. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Credit Agreement Obligations</U>&#148; shall mean the Obligations (as defined in the Credit Agreement). </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Event of Default</U>&#148; &#150; an &#147;Event of Default&#148; under any Secured Instrument. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Indenture Trustee</U>&#148; &#150; as defined in the <U>second recital</U>. </FONT></P>

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Lender</U>&#148; &#150; as defined in the <U>first recital</U>. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Obligors</U>&#148; &#150; as defined in the <U>preamble</U>. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Opinion of Counsel</U>&#148; shall mean an opinion in writing signed by legal counsel (other than an employee of the Borrower or any Affiliate thereof) satisfactory to the Collateral Agent, who may be counsel
regularly retained by the Collateral Agent. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>PATRIOT Act</U>&#148; means the USA PATRIOT Act (Title III of Pub. L. 107-56 (signed
into law October&nbsp;26, 2001)), as amended or otherwise modified from time to time. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>PATRIOT Act Disclosures</U>&#148; means all
documentation and other information which the Collateral Agent reasonably requests in order to comply with its ongoing obligations under applicable &#147;know your customer&#148; and anti-money laundering rules and regulations, including the PATRIOT
Act. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Proceeds</U>&#148; shall mean all proceeds within the meaning of the UCC. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Requisite Instructions</U>&#148; shall mean written instructions of the Control Party; <U>provided</U> that such instructions shall include a
certification from the Control Party that the action contemplated by such instructions is permitted under the applicable Security Documents (including, if, pursuant to such Security Documents, such action is permitted to be taken only during the
existence of a Default or Event of Default, a certification that such Default or Event of Default exists). </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Secured
Instruments</U>&#148; shall mean the Credit Agreement, the other Loan Documents, the 2012 Notes Indenture and the 2012 Notes. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman"
SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Secured Obligations</U>&#148; shall mean, without duplication, (i)&nbsp;the Credit Agreement Obligations, (ii)&nbsp;the 2012 Notes, and (iii)&nbsp;all sums payable by the Borrower under this Agreement or any Security Document
(including Collateral Agent Fees). </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Secured Parties</U>&#148; shall mean at any time the holders of the Secured Obligations.
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Security Documents</U>&#148; shall mean this Agreement and each other agreement pursuant to which the Collateral Agent is granted
a Lien to secure the Secured Obligations (including each agreement entered into pursuant to <U>clause (b)(ii)</U> of <U>Section&nbsp;6.3</U> of this Agreement). </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Termination Date</U>&#148; shall mean the date on which the Loans and all other Obligations (other than contingent surviving indemnity obligations in respect of which no claim or demand has been made and
Hedging Obligations under Specified Hedge Agreements) have been paid in full, all commitments to extend credit under the Loan Documents have terminated and any Hedging Obligations under the Specified Hedge Agreements owed to any Qualified
Counterparty shall have either (i)&nbsp;paid in full or (ii)&nbsp;secured by a collateral arrangement satisfactory to the Qualified Counterparty in its sole discretion. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;<U>Trust Estate</U>&#148; shall have the meaning assigned in the Declaration of Trust in this Agreement. </FONT></P>

</I></B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><I>First Platform Solutions in 2007&#151; </I></B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT
FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><I>&#151;Unveils &#147;Fusion&#148; Initiative for Industry&#146;s First Integrated CPU/GPU </I></B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman"
SIZE="2"><B><I>Silicon Solutions&#151; </I></B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>SUNNYVALE, CALIF. &#151; Oct. 25, 2006 &#151; </B>AMD (NYSE:AMD) today announced the
completion of its approximately $5.4 billion acquisition of ATI Technologies Inc. Combining the complementary strengths of the two technology leaders, the new AMD opens for business as a processing powerhouse committed to driving innovation, choice
and growth in the technology industry. With approximately 15,000 employees, the company merges AMD&#146;s technology leadership in microprocessors together with ATI&#146;s leadership in graphics, chipsets and consumer electronics. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;Today marks a historic day for our employees, our partners and our customers as we officially welcome ATI into the AMD family,&#148; said AMD
Chairman and CEO Hector Ruiz. &#147;On day one, we are delivering a winning set of complementary technologies, igniting a new level of innovation and continuing to champion choice for the industry. Thanks to the strength of our talented employees,
the new AMD now has a full range of intellectual property (IP) in microprocessors, graphics, chipsets and consumer electronics to deliver open platforms and integrated solutions. In the near term, customers gain a new level of choice, and in the
long term, we believe the possibilities for innovation are truly limitless.&#148; </FONT></P>

<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" COLOR="#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><I>Transaction Details </I></B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Under the terms of the transaction, AMD acquired all of the outstanding common shares of ATI for a combination of approximately $4.3 billion in cash and 58&nbsp;million shares of AMD common stock, based on the number
of shares of ATI common stock outstanding on October&nbsp;24, 2006. All outstanding options and restricted stock units (RSUs) of ATI were assumed. The value of the ATI acquisition of approximately $5.4 billion is based upon the closing stock price
of AMD common stock on October&nbsp;24, 2006 of $20.32 per share and excludes the value of assumed equity awards. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">AMD financed the cash
portion of the transaction with a combination of cash and new debt. AMD obtained a $2.5 billion term loan from Morgan Stanley Senior Funding, Inc., which, together with combined existing cash, cash equivalents, and marketable securities balances of
approximately $1.8 billion, provided full funding for the transaction. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">AMD announced the final pro-ration applicable to ATI common shares
in the acquisition. The total consideration to be paid for each common share, based on the Parent Closing Stock Price (as defined in the Plan of Arrangement, as amended), is approximately $21.36. The final election results indicate that pro-ration
is as follows: </FONT></P> <P STYLE="font-size:6px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="4%"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></TD>
<TD WIDTH="2%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#149;</FONT></TD>
<TD WIDTH="1%" VALIGN="top"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">ATI shareholders who elected to receive cash will be entitled to receive, for each common share for which a valid cash election was made, approximately US $18.59 in cash plus
approximately 0.1245 of a share of AMD common stock; </FONT></TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="font-size:6px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">

and </FONT></TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="font-size:6px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>

</FONT></TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Pro-ration was necessary because the cash consideration elected to be received exceeded the amount of cash available in the
acquisition. Any fractional shares will be paid in cash. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><I>Planning to Deliver Integrated Platforms in 2007 </I></B></FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Customers should benefit from AMD&#146;s and ATI&#146;s combined platform development and technical support teams, which will be co-located in Taipei and
Shanghai. Combined with the existing Austin and Toronto locations, these sites offer research and development and support to provide customers with a complete solution for optimized platform development. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2 </FONT></P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" COLOR="#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">AMD plans to deliver a range of integrated platforms in 2007 to serve key markets, including: commercial
clients; mobile computing; and gaming and media computing. PC users will benefit from innovations intended to extend battery life on the next-generation AMD Turion&#153; 64 mobile technology-based platform and enhancements to the AMD LIVE!&#153;
digital media PC platform that will enable users to get more from their favorite photos, music, and movies. AMD believes that these integrated platform innovations will bring customers improved system stability, better time-to-market, increased
performance and energy-efficiency and overall, an enhanced user experience. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;By driving innovation and integration in processing,
especially in graphics, the new AMD has the potential to empower breakthrough computing experiences for users of Windows<FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="1" COLOR="#000000"><SUP>&reg;<FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2"
COLOR="#000000"></FONT></SUP><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2" COLOR="#000000"></FONT></FONT> Vista,&#153;&#148; said Jim Allchin, Co-President of Microsoft&#146;s Platforms&nbsp;&amp; Services Division. &#147;We are excited by the potential
benefits that this union can bring to enhance the Windows Vista experience.&#148; </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">AMD also sees an opportunity to deliver processing
solutions to the growing consumer electronics market. The company intends to leverage ATI&#146;s strength in the consumer market by pursuing new opportunities to invest in the consumer electronics and high-end discrete graphics markets. With leading
technology and customer relationships, AMD is positioned to address digital convergence by leveraging critical IP to create new innovations and devices that facilitate end-to-end content delivery and connectivity to improve end-user experiences.
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><I>CPU/GPU Silicon &#147;Fusion&#148; &#150; Another Industry First for Customers </I></B></FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">AMD plans to create a new class of x86 processor that integrates the central processing unit (CPU) and graphics processing unit (GPU) at the silicon level
with a broad set of design initiatives collectively codenamed &#147;Fusion.&#148; AMD intends to design Fusion processors to provide step-function increases in performance-per-watt relative to today&#146;s CPU-only architectures, and to provide the
best customer experience in a world increasingly reliant upon 3D graphics, digital media and high-performance computing. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">With Fusion
processors, AMD will continue to promote an open platform and encourage companies throughout the ecosystem to create innovative new co-processing solutions aimed at further optimizing specific workloads. AMD-powered Fusion platforms will continue to
fully support high-end discrete graphics, physics accelerators, and other PCI Express-based solutions to meet the ever-increasing needs of the most demanding enthusiast end-users. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT
SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">3 </FONT></P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" COLOR="#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;With the anticipated launch of Windows Vista, robust 3D graphics, digital media and device
convergence are driving the need for greater performance, graphics capabilities, and battery life,&#148; said Phil Hester, AMD senior vice president and chief&nbsp;technology officer. &#147;In this increasingly diverse x86 computing environment,
simply adding more CPU cores to a baseline architecture will not be enough.&nbsp;As x86 scales from palmtops to petaFLOPS, modular processor designs leveraging both CPU and GPU compute capabilities will be essential in meeting the requirements of
computing in 2008 and beyond.&#148; </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Fusion processors are expected in late 2008/early 2009, and the company expects to use them within all
of the company&#146;s priority computing categories, including laptops, desktops, workstations and servers, as well as in consumer electronics and solutions tailored for the unique needs of emerging markets. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><I>About AMD </I></B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Advanced Micro Devices (NYSE: AMD)
is a leading global provider of innovative processing solutions in the computing, graphics and consumer electronics markets. AMD is dedicated to driving open innovation, choice and industry growth by delivering superior customer-centric solutions
that empower&nbsp;consumers and businesses worldwide. For more information, visit www.amd.com. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><I>Safe Harbor Statement </I></B></FONT></P> <P

ALIGN="center"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>QUOTE ADDENDUM </B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>NVIDIA Corporation </B></FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;NVIDIA and AMD have innovated together for over a decade. As a result,&nbsp;NVIDIA GeForce and nForce processors set&nbsp;the standard for AMD
systems. AMD&#146;s open platform strategy enables continued partnership aimed at providing customers with a wide choice of&nbsp;industry leading solutions,&#148;&nbsp;said Dan Vivoli, executive vice president, NVIDIA Corporation. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>DELL </B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">&#147;Dell is focused on enhancing our
